<TSO name="greece" version="1" chance_for_no_tso="35" >
	<!-- these are objects placed on the corners, the chance here is all relative, so basically you can make it favour some objects over others for corners! -->
	<!-- a zero chance means it wont get selected, but it may be the object is included for barriers/drops etc etc where it will be favoured for that placement -->
	<!--a sub object of an object means, place these using the currently placed object as a base, and only place between the min and max of that object! -->
	<Object binaryfile="terrainobjects" xmlsource="Armco" 			object="basic_armco"						chance="5" 	/>
	<Object binaryfile="terrainobjects" xmlsource="woodenpole" 		object="yellow_tape" 						chance="30" > 		
		<Object	binaryfile="terrainobjects" xmlsource="people" 		object="corner_spectators"/>
	</Object>
	<Object	binaryfile="terrainobjects" xmlsource="people" 			object="corner_spectators_behind_fence"		chance="30"	/>	
	<Object binaryfile="terrainobjects" xmlsource="Barriers" 		object="corners" 							chance="60" />
	<Object binaryfile="terrainobjects" xmlsource="AdvertBoards" 	object="corners" 							chance="40" />
	<Object binaryfile="terrainobjects" xmlsource="MeshFence" 		object="meshfence" 							chance="30" />
	<Object binaryfile="terrainobjects" xmlsource="Bollard" 		object="black_square"						chance="40" />
</TSO>
